title: Affine research policy factory isolation and final acceptance
tool: Source-bound Swift release build, native catalogue and isolated policy compiler
---
The initial isolated-policy repair moves the research helper body out of the pure configuration declaration, allowing the standalone context compiler to pass. Exact-head engine and app CI then correctly refuse that moved body because it cannot mutate ModelConfig private setters from another source file. Both compiler failures remain preserved. The earlier isolated proxy pass never established full-module compilation.

The final repair retains private setters. A pure typed recipe-copy method lives inside ModelConfig; the artifact-bound research extension constructs the two authenticated projection descriptors and delegates the copy. No model-I/O or research-artifact dependency enters the isolated policy source, and no setter becomes public or package writable. Record geometry, admitted artifact identity and numerical operations stay unchanged.

The full release build succeeds. Its frozen source-bound identity is retained below. All 104 native T0/T1 groups pass with zero failures or skips, and the existing independent policy compiler passes all 965,028 assertions without MLX or model allocation. This source is committed as 20cdda3b69c0adcd76c865e0125f33f5deb67fa9. Full earlier static acceptance is reused within its unchanged instrument/transport scope, not described as freshly rerun. New-head remote CI remains pending here. Performance/quality observations remain tied to their original frozen binary; the factory separation is not a new performance result.
